## Baby Bounce

- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

A first storytime experience to share with babies (ages 0-18 months who are not yet walking) and their caregivers. Each week we will share a short story with our babies, listen to music, and work on our bounces and rhymes! Toddlers that are walking and preschoolers are invited to join us at SMART on Monday mornings. Library staff may take photos, videos, or audio recordings at our events. Please tell us if you do not wish to be photographed, video-recorded, or audio-recorded. We will always respect your requests. An ASL (American Sign Language) interpreter or closed captioning can be provided for this program if requested at least seven business days in advance. For assistance, please contact your library or accessibility@aclibrary.org accessibility@aclibrary.org.

Project Eden Youth Prevention Services provides general information, education, and supportive materials for youth, young adults, and parents on making healthy choices, creating a safe environment for discussing substance misuse, and exploring healthy alternatives. Feel free to stop by with any questions regarding our services, projects, or future parent workshops. Library staff may take photos, videos, or audio recordings at our events. Please tell us if you do not wish to be photographed, video-recorded, or audio-recorded. We will always respect your requests. An ASL (American Sign Language) interpreter or closed captioning can be provided for this program if requested at least seven (7) days in advance. - tickets:

Combining blistering dobro and gritty lyrics, Matt Bradford weaves instrumental mastery and heartfelt songwriting into a genre-defying blend of Folk, Blues, Americana, Country, and Pop. The savvy listener will detect echoes of a wide range of artists from Townes Van Zandt to Bruce Springsteen, and Jeffrey Foucault to Chris Whitley. But ultimately, it is distinctly Matt Bradford. Matt's music is available on itunes and spotify. https://itunes.apple.com/us/artist/matt-bradford/id552581869

## Free Social Adults-Only (19+) Chess Club

- id: d62475862b8e
- slug: free-social-adults-only-19-chess-club-d62475
- date: 2026-09-10
- when: Thursday, September 10, 2026, 5:00 PM
- start: 2026-09-10T17:00:00-07:00
- end: 
- all_day: no
- venue: Pacific Pearl Mall Food Hall (next to Shihlin Taiwan Street Food Snacks)
- address: Pleasanton, CA
- city: Pleasanton
- category: family
- tags: 
- price: 
- free: yes
- family_friendly: yes
- url: https://www.pleasantonweekly.com/calendar/#!/details/free-social-adults-only-19-chess-club/16159390/2026-09-10T17
- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Our weekly casual chess nights are aimed at adults (19+) of all levels, including: - passionate online players looking to connect and compete in person - chess parents that want to keep up with their kids - novices that are curious about the game - come between 5:30-6:30 PM to get instruction and tips! We welcome women and seniors! - current and returning tournament players. Join us if you are looking for a non-intimidating way to exercise your brain, hang with other adults, and maybe even win a prize! Organizer: Emerald Hills Chess is a Dublin nonprofit that teaches 300 K-12 students in the Tri-Valley, and also holds popular (100-150 player) US Chess-rated monthly tournaments.

## Music lessons with Pleasanton's 1776-era fife and drum children's band

- id: 42842e50ee62

- date: 2026-09-18
- when: Friday, September 18, 2026, 5:00 PM
- start: 2026-09-18T17:00:00-07:00
- end: 
- all_day: no
- venue: near Mohr Elementary school, call for details
- address: Pleasanton, CA
- city: Pleasanton
- category: music
- tags: 
- price: 
- free: no
- family_friendly: no
- url: https://www.pleasantonweekly.com/calendar/#!/details/music-lessons-with-pleasanton-s-1776-era-fife-and-drum-children-s-band/19689145/2026-09-18T17
- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Do you want your child (8-16) to experience the fun, education and social camaraderie of being in the marching band---but without the enormous time commitment of a school marching band? Look no further! We have been providing music and leadership education to Pleasanton-area kids since our first lesson on March 19th 2010. The Young American Patriots Fife & Drum Corps "YAPs" is a NON-POLITICAL children's parade band dedicated to perpetuating the music, history & heritage of our nation's founding through musical education & traditional value-based teaching. We teach fife (like a flute) and drum in a small group setting.

By popular demand, City Center Bishop Ranch, San Ramon’s lively open-air dining, shopping and entertainment destination, is bringing back its signature restaurant week, Taste of City Center, Sept. 18-27, with casual eats, elevated dinners and exclusive deals across the destination's acclaimed dining lineup. Savor the flavors of the season during Taste of City Center—our very own restaurant week celebrating every delicious bite our eateries have to offer. From quick bites to leisurely dinners, enjoy special prix fixe menus, limited-time dishes, and curated pairings from all your favorite spots. Spend $300 at participating restaurants between September 18-27 and receive a free ticket to September Sips!

We welcome women and seniors! - current and returning tournament players. Join us if you are looking for a non-intimidating way to exercise your brain, hang with other adults, and maybe even win a prize! Organizer: Emerald Hills Chess is a Dublin nonprofit that teaches 300 K-12 students in the Tri-Valley, and also holds popular (100-150 player) US Chess-rated monthly tournaments.

Biz Links is a Chamber networking group focused on building meaningful business relationships, increasing visibility, exchanging referrals, and supporting fellow professionals in the community. Members enjoy valuable networking, engaging conversation, and opportunities to grow both personally and professionally in a welcoming environment. Biz Links meets on the 2nd Tuesday of each month at 12:00 PM at the Pleasanton Chamber of Commerce. Chamber membership is required to join the group, but guests are always welcome to come check us out. Whether you're a longtime member or new to networking, Biz Links is a great place to connect, build relationships, and grow with other local professionals.

## Music lessons with Pleasanton's 1776-era fife and drum children's band

- id: 17fe9bd8389c
- slug: music-lessons-with-pleasanton-s-1776-era-fife-and-drum-children-s-band-17fe9b
- date: 2026-10-16
- when: Friday, October 16, 2026, 5:00 PM
- start: 2026-10-16T17:00:00-07:00
- end: 
- all_day: no
- venue: near Mohr Elementary school, call for details
- address: Pleasanton, CA
- city: Pleasanton
- category: music
- tags: 
- price: 
- free: no
- family_friendly: no
- url: https://www.pleasantonweekly.com/calendar/#!/details/music-lessons-with-pleasanton-s-1776-era-fife-and-drum-children-s-band/19689145/2026-10-16T17
- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Do you want your child (8-16) to experience the fun, education and social camaraderie of being in the marching band---but without the enormous time commitment of a school marching band? Look no further! We have been providing music and leadership education to Pleasanton-area kids since our first lesson on March 19th 2010. The Young American Patriots Fife & Drum Corps "YAPs" is a NON-POLITICAL children's parade band dedicated to perpetuating the music, history & heritage of our nation's founding through musical education & traditional value-based teaching. We teach fife (like a flute) and drum in a small group setting.

For 53 years the nation&'s most talented artisans have successfully sold their creations at the Harvest Festival® Original Art & Craft Shows. We have seven established venues that attract thousands of eager shoppers with their "shop till you drop" attitude, looking for their next treasures to purchase. Don't miss your opportunity to join us on our 2026 tour. No application or jury fee. Category limitations. No commission on sales. Indoor booths in climate-controlled facilities, hotel discounts, exhibitor shipping, 24 hr. security. No buy & sell, commercial, or imported products allowed in our artisan booths. Extensive marketing in all medias.3.5 mi

## Free Social Adults-Only (19+) Chess Club

- category: family
- tags:

- free: yes

- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Our weekly casual chess nights are aimed at adults (19+) of all levels, including: - passionate online players looking to connect and compete in person - chess parents that want to keep up with their kids - novices that are curious about the game - come between 5:30-6:30 PM to get instruction and tips! We welcome women and seniors! - current and returning tournament players. Join us if you are looking for a non-intimidating way to exercise your brain, hang with other adults, and maybe even win a prize! Organizer: Emerald Hills Chess is a Dublin nonprofit that teaches 300 K-12 students in the Tri-Valley, and also holds popular (100-150 player) US Chess-rated monthly tournaments.

Biz Links is a Chamber networking group focused on building meaningful business relationships, increasing visibility, exchanging referrals, and supporting fellow professionals in the community. Members enjoy valuable networking, engaging conversation, and opportunities to grow both personally and professionally in a welcoming environment. Biz Links meets on the 2nd Tuesday of each month at 12:00 PM at the Pleasanton Chamber of Commerce. Chamber membership is required to join the group, but guests are always welcome to come check us out. Whether you're a longtime member or new to networking, Biz Links is a great place to connect, build relationships, and grow with other local professionals.

## Music lessons with Pleasanton's 1776-era fife and drum children's band

- id: e5c5c84ddc07
- slug: music-lessons-with-pleasanton-s-1776-era-fife-and-drum-children-s-band-e5c5c8
- date: 2026-11-13
- when: Friday, November 13, 2026, 4:00 PM
- start: 2026-11-13T17:00:00-07:00
- end: 
- all_day: no
- venue: near Mohr Elementary school, call for details
- address: Pleasanton, CA
- city: Pleasanton
- category: music
- tags: 
- price: 
- free: no
- family_friendly: no
- url: https://www.pleasantonweekly.com/calendar/#!/details/music-lessons-with-pleasanton-s-1776-era-fife-and-drum-children-s-band/19689145/2026-11-13T17
- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Do you want your child (8-16) to experience the fun, education and social camaraderie of being in the marching band---but without the enormous time commitment of a school marching band? Look no further! We have been providing music and leadership education to Pleasanton-area kids since our first lesson on March 19th 2010. The Young American Patriots Fife & Drum Corps "YAPs" is a NON-POLITICAL children's parade band dedicated to perpetuating the music, history & heritage of our nation's founding through musical education & traditional value-based teaching. We teach fife (like a flute) and drum in a small group setting.

Mark Bettencourt and the Aftermath is probably the highest energy band you will ever see. Lead vocalist Mark Bettencourt brilliantly covers songs that you forgot you loved! The Band hails from the now famous Silicon Valley. You may have experienced the Aftermath at one of their many prestigious events such as: The San Jose Grand Prix, Rotary club events, Street Fairs, Corporate events, Yacht clubs as well a many local music bars and night clubs. But no matter where you choose to come experience the band, you will be guaranteed to be taken back to a place where you can unplug and just enjoy yourself! ## LARPD Puzzle Palooza

- id: a02d15c27070
- slug: larpd-puzzle-palooza-a02d15
- date: 2026-11-22
- when: Sunday, November 22, 2026, 12:00 PM
- start: 2026-11-22T13:00:00-07:00
- end: 
- all_day: no
- venue: Livermore Aquatic Center
- address: Livermore, CA
- city: Livermore
- category: festival
- tags: 
- price: 
- free: no
- family_friendly: yes
- url: https://www.pleasantonweekly.com/calendar/#!/details/larpd-puzzle-palooza/19733192/2026-11-22T13
- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

There will be 3 hours to complete a 1000-, 750-, or 500-piece puzzle, with a prize for the first team to finish! Register a team of up to four people to compete in the ultimate puzzle challenge. Upon arrival, teams will receive the mystery puzzle and a table to work on. Light refreshments and snacks will be available, and all teams get to keep their puzzles at the end of the competition. Only one team member needs to register. $48 Resident/$53 Non-resident per team Pick Your Puzzle Size: 1000, 750, 500 pieces Register Online at: https://www.larpd.org/puzzle-palooza-fall Kids of all ages are welcome! An adult must accompany children under 13.

- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Our weekly casual chess nights are aimed at adults (19+) of all levels, including: - passionate online players looking to connect and compete in person - chess parents that want to keep up with their kids - novices that are curious about the game - come between 5:30-6:30 PM to get instruction and tips! We welcome women and seniors! - current and returning tournament players. Join us if you are looking for a non-intimidating way to exercise your brain, hang with other adults, and maybe even win a prize! Organizer: Emerald Hills Chess is a Dublin nonprofit that teaches 300 K-12 students in the Tri-Valley, and also holds popular (100-150 player) US Chess-rated monthly tournaments.

## Music lessons with Pleasanton's 1776-era fife and drum children's band

- id: 79d708ff5e08
- slug: music-lessons-with-pleasanton-s-1776-era-fife-and-drum-children-s-band-79d708
- date: 2026-11-27
- when: Friday, November 27, 2026, 4:00 PM
- start: 2026-11-27T17:00:00-07:00
- end: 
- all_day: no
- venue: near Mohr Elementary school, call for details
- address: Pleasanton, CA
- city: Pleasanton
- category: music
- tags: 
- price: 
- free: no
- family_friendly: no
- url: https://www.pleasantonweekly.com/calendar/#!/details/music-lessons-with-pleasanton-s-1776-era-fife-and-drum-children-s-band/19689145/2026-11-27T17
- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Do you want your child (8-16) to experience the fun, education and social camaraderie of being in the marching band---but without the enormous time commitment of a school marching band? Look no further! We have been providing music and leadership education to Pleasanton-area kids since our first lesson on March 19th 2010. The Young American Patriots Fife & Drum Corps "YAPs" is a NON-POLITICAL children's parade band dedicated to perpetuating the music, history & heritage of our nation's founding through musical education & traditional value-based teaching. We teach fife (like a flute) and drum in a small group setting.

- tags:

- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Our weekly casual chess nights are aimed at adults (19+) of all levels, including: - passionate online players looking to connect and compete in person - chess parents that want to keep up with their kids - novices that are curious about the game - come between 5:30-6:30 PM to get instruction and tips! We welcome women and seniors! - current and returning tournament players. Join us if you are looking for a non-intimidating way to exercise your brain, hang with other adults, and maybe even win a prize! Organizer: Emerald Hills Chess is a Dublin nonprofit that teaches 300 K-12 students in the Tri-Valley, and also holds popular (100-150 player) US Chess-rated monthly tournaments.

Biz Links is a Chamber networking group focused on building meaningful business relationships, increasing visibility, exchanging referrals, and supporting fellow professionals in the community. Members enjoy valuable networking, engaging conversation, and opportunities to grow both personally and professionally in a welcoming environment. Biz Links meets on the 2nd Tuesday of each month at 12:00 PM at the Pleasanton Chamber of Commerce. Chamber membership is required to join the group, but guests are always welcome to come check us out. Whether you're a longtime member or new to networking, Biz Links is a great place to connect, build relationships, and grow with other local professionals.

## Music lessons with Pleasanton's 1776-era fife and drum children's band

- id: a58ce3bc962a
- slug: music-lessons-with-pleasanton-s-1776-era-fife-and-drum-children-s-band-a58ce3
- date: 2026-12-11
- when: Friday, December 11, 2026, 4:00 PM
- start: 2026-12-11T17:00:00-07:00
- end: 
- all_day: no
- venue: near Mohr Elementary school, call for details
- address: Pleasanton, CA
- city: Pleasanton
- category: music
- tags: 
- price: 
- free: no
- family_friendly: no
- url: https://www.pleasantonweekly.com/calendar/#!/details/music-lessons-with-pleasanton-s-1776-era-fife-and-drum-children-s-band/19689145/2026-12-11T17
- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Do you want your child (8-16) to experience the fun, education and social camaraderie of being in the marching band---but without the enormous time commitment of a school marching band? Look no further! We have been providing music and leadership education to Pleasanton-area kids since our first lesson on March 19th 2010. The Young American Patriots Fife & Drum Corps "YAPs" is a NON-POLITICAL children's parade band dedicated to perpetuating the music, history & heritage of our nation's founding through musical education & traditional value-based teaching. We teach fife (like a flute) and drum in a small group setting.

## Andy Grammer: Greater Than Pt. II: A One Man Show

- id: dd74d1ca1e8f
- slug: andy-grammer-greater-than-pt-ii-a-one-man-show-dd74d1
- date: 2026-12-12
- when: Saturday, December 12, 2026, 7:00 PM
- start: 2026-12-12T20:00:00-07:00
- end: 
- all_day: no
- venue: Dougherty Valley Performing Arts Center
- address: San Ramon, CA
- city: San Ramon
- category: music
- tags: 
- price: 
- free: no
- family_friendly: no
- url: https://www.pleasantonweekly.com/calendar/#!/details/andy-grammer-greater-than-pt-ii-a-one-man-show/19678025/2026-12-12T20
- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

An intimate, high-energy evening with multi-platinum artist Andy Grammer. Stripped to its core, this one-man show blends acoustic performance, spoken word, storytelling, and real-time audience connection into something deeply personal and undeniably uplifting. With only a piano, a guitar, and a room full of voices, Andy turns the stage into a shared experience: moving effortlessly between chart- topping hits, new songs, and the stories behind them. Raw, funny, and fully present, it’s an invitation into the heart of the music.

## Free Social Adults-Only (19+) Chess Club

- id: acea3e93d98a
- slug: free-social-adults-only-19-chess-club-acea3e
- date: 2026-12-24
- when: Thursday, December 24, 2026, 4:00 PM
- start: 2026-12-24T17:00:00-07:00
- end: 
- all_day: no
- venue: Pacific Pearl Mall Food Hall (next to Shihlin Taiwan Street Food Snacks)
- address: Pleasanton, CA
- city: Pleasanton
- category: family
- tags: 
- price: 
- free: yes
- family_friendly: yes
- url: https://www.pleasantonweekly.com/calendar/#!/details/free-social-adults-only-19-chess-club/16159390/2026-12-24T17
- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Our weekly casual chess nights are aimed at adults (19+) of all levels, including: - passionate online players looking to connect and compete in person - chess parents that want to keep up with their kids - novices that are curious about the game - come between 5:30-6:30 PM to get instruction and tips! We welcome women and seniors! - current and returning tournament players. Join us if you are looking for a non-intimidating way to exercise your brain, hang with other adults, and maybe even win a prize! Organizer: Emerald Hills Chess is a Dublin nonprofit that teaches 300 K-12 students in the Tri-Valley, and also holds popular (100-150 player) US Chess-rated monthly tournaments.

## Music lessons with Pleasanton's 1776-era fife and drum children's band

- id: c383d8a443c1
- slug: music-lessons-with-pleasanton-s-1776-era-fife-and-drum-children-s-band-c383d8
- date: 2026-12-25
- when: Friday, December 25, 2026, 4:00 PM
- start: 2026-12-25T17:00:00-07:00
- end: 
- all_day: no
- venue: near Mohr Elementary school, call for details
- address: Pleasanton, CA
- city: Pleasanton
- category: music
- tags: 
- price: 
- free: no
- family_friendly: no
- url: https://www.pleasantonweekly.com/calendar/#!/details/music-lessons-with-pleasanton-s-1776-era-fife-and-drum-children-s-band/19689145/2026-12-25T17
- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Do you want your child (8-16) to experience the fun, education and social camaraderie of being in the marching band---but without the enormous time commitment of a school marching band? Look no further! We have been providing music and leadership education to Pleasanton-area kids since our first lesson on March 19th 2010. The Young American Patriots Fife & Drum Corps "YAPs" is a NON-POLITICAL children's parade band dedicated to perpetuating the music, history & heritage of our nation's founding through musical education & traditional value-based teaching. We teach fife (like a flute) and drum in a small group setting.

- start: 2026-12-31T17:00:00-07:00
- end: 
- all_day: no
- venue: Pacific Pearl Mall Food Hall (next to Shihlin Taiwan Street Food Snacks)
- address: Pleasanton, CA
- city: Pleasanton
- category: family
- tags: 
- price: 
- free: yes
- family_friendly: yes
- url: https://www.pleasantonweekly.com/calendar/#!/details/free-social-adults-only-19-chess-club/16159390/2026-12-31T17
- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Our weekly casual chess nights are aimed at adults (19+) of all levels, including: - passionate online players looking to connect and compete in person - chess parents that want to keep up with their kids - novices that are curious about the game - come between 5:30-6:30 PM to get instruction and tips! We welcome women and seniors! - current and returning tournament players. Join us if you are looking for a non-intimidating way to exercise your brain, hang with other adults, and maybe even win a prize! Organizer: Emerald Hills Chess is a Dublin nonprofit that teaches 300 K-12 students in the Tri-Valley, and also holds popular (100-150 player) US Chess-rated monthly tournaments.

## Music lessons with Pleasanton's 1776-era fife and drum children's band

- id: 92a082ace339
- slug: music-lessons-with-pleasanton-s-1776-era-fife-and-drum-children-s-band-92a082
- date: 2027-01-08
- when: Friday, January 8, 2027, 4:00 PM
- start: 2027-01-08T17:00:00-07:00
- end: 
- all_day: no
- venue: near Mohr Elementary school, call for details
- address: Pleasanton, CA
- city: Pleasanton
- category: music
- tags: 
- price: 
- free: no
- family_friendly: no
- url: https://www.pleasantonweekly.com/calendar/#!/details/music-lessons-with-pleasanton-s-1776-era-fife-and-drum-children-s-band/19689145/2027-01-08T17
- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Do you want your child (8-16) to experience the fun, education and social camaraderie of being in the marching band---but without the enormous time commitment of a school marching band? Look no further! We have been providing music and leadership education to Pleasanton-area kids since our first lesson on March 19th 2010. The Young American Patriots Fife & Drum Corps "YAPs" is a NON-POLITICAL children's parade band dedicated to perpetuating the music, history & heritage of our nation's founding through musical education & traditional value-based teaching. We teach fife (like a flute) and drum in a small group setting.

- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Biz Links is a Chamber networking group focused on building meaningful business relationships, increasing visibility, exchanging referrals, and supporting fellow professionals in the community. Members enjoy valuable networking, engaging conversation, and opportunities to grow both personally and professionally in a welcoming environment. Biz Links meets on the 2nd Tuesday of each month at 12:00 PM at the Pleasanton Chamber of Commerce. Chamber membership is required to join the group, but guests are always welcome to come check us out. Whether you're a longtime member or new to networking, Biz Links is a great place to connect, build relationships, and grow with other local professionals.

- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Our weekly casual chess nights are aimed at adults (19+) of all levels, including: - passionate online players looking to connect and compete in person - chess parents that want to keep up with their kids - novices that are curious about the game - come between 5:30-6:30 PM to get instruction and tips! We welcome women and seniors! - current and returning tournament players. Join us if you are looking for a non-intimidating way to exercise your brain, hang with other adults, and maybe even win a prize! Organizer: Emerald Hills Chess is a Dublin nonprofit that teaches 300 K-12 students in the Tri-Valley, and also holds popular (100-150 player) US Chess-rated monthly tournaments.

## Music lessons with Pleasanton's 1776-era fife and drum children's band

- id: 8c86578b2481
- slug: music-lessons-with-pleasanton-s-1776-era-fife-and-drum-children-s-band-8c8657
- date: 2027-01-22
- when: Friday, January 22, 2027, 4:00 PM
- start: 2027-01-22T17:00:00-07:00
- end: 
- all_day: no
- venue: near Mohr Elementary school, call for details
- address: Pleasanton, CA
- city: Pleasanton
- category: music
- tags: 
- price: 
- free: no
- family_friendly: no
- url: https://www.pleasantonweekly.com/calendar/#!/details/music-lessons-with-pleasanton-s-1776-era-fife-and-drum-children-s-band/19689145/2027-01-22T17
- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Do you want your child (8-16) to experience the fun, education and social camaraderie of being in the marching band---but without the enormous time commitment of a school marching band? Look no further! We have been providing music and leadership education to Pleasanton-area kids since our first lesson on March 19th 2010. The Young American Patriots Fife & Drum Corps "YAPs" is a NON-POLITICAL children's parade band dedicated to perpetuating the music, history & heritage of our nation's founding through musical education & traditional value-based teaching. We teach fife (like a flute) and drum in a small group setting.

- category: family
- tags: 
- price: 
- free: yes
- family_friendly: yes
- url: https://www.pleasantonweekly.com/calendar/#!/details/free-social-adults-only-19-chess-club/16159390/2027-02-04T17
- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Our weekly casual chess nights are aimed at adults (19+) of all levels, including: - passionate online players looking to connect and compete in person - chess parents that want to keep up with their kids - novices that are curious about the game - come between 5:30-6:30 PM to get instruction and tips! We welcome women and seniors! - current and returning tournament players. Join us if you are looking for a non-intimidating way to exercise your brain, hang with other adults, and maybe even win a prize! Organizer: Emerald Hills Chess is a Dublin nonprofit that teaches 300 K-12 students in the Tri-Valley, and also holds popular (100-150 player) US Chess-rated monthly tournaments.

## Music lessons with Pleasanton's 1776-era fife and drum children's band

- id: b7c2c099cac8
- slug: music-lessons-with-pleasanton-s-1776-era-fife-and-drum-children-s-band-b7c2c0
- date: 2027-02-05
- when: Friday, February 5, 2027, 4:00 PM
- start: 2027-02-05T17:00:00-07:00
- end: 
- all_day: no
- venue: near Mohr Elementary school, call for details
- address: Pleasanton, CA
- city: Pleasanton
- category: music
- tags: 
- price: 
- free: no
- family_friendly: no
- url: https://www.pleasantonweekly.com/calendar/#!/details/music-lessons-with-pleasanton-s-1776-era-fife-and-drum-children-s-band/19689145/2027-02-05T17
- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Do you want your child (8-16) to experience the fun, education and social camaraderie of being in the marching band---but without the enormous time commitment of a school marching band? Look no further! We have been providing music and leadership education to Pleasanton-area kids since our first lesson on March 19th 2010. The Young American Patriots Fife & Drum Corps "YAPs" is a NON-POLITICAL children's parade band dedicated to perpetuating the music, history & heritage of our nation's founding through musical education & traditional value-based teaching. We teach fife (like a flute) and drum in a small group setting.

## Biz Links Networking Group

- id: 5bb66defc074
- slug: biz-links-networking-group-5bb66d

- when: Tuesday, February 9, 2027, 11:00 AM
- start: 2027-02-09T12:00:00-07:00
- end:

- address: Pleasanton, CA
- city: Pleasanton
- category: other
- tags: 
- price:

- url: https://www.pleasantonweekly.com/calendar/#!/details/biz-links-networking-group/19006731/2027-02-09T12
- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Biz Links is a Chamber networking group focused on building meaningful business relationships, increasing visibility, exchanging referrals, and supporting fellow professionals in the community. Members enjoy valuable networking, engaging conversation, and opportunities to grow both personally and professionally in a welcoming environment. Biz Links meets on the 2nd Tuesday of each month at 12:00 PM at the Pleasanton Chamber of Commerce. Chamber membership is required to join the group, but guests are always welcome to come check us out. Whether you're a longtime member or new to networking, Biz Links is a great place to connect, build relationships, and grow with other local professionals.

Our weekly casual chess nights are aimed at adults (19+) of all levels, including: - passionate online players looking to connect and compete in person - chess parents that want to keep up with their kids - novices that are curious about the game - come between 5:30-6:30 PM to get instruction and tips! We welcome women and seniors! - current and returning tournament players. Join us if you are looking for a non-intimidating way to exercise your brain, hang with other adults, and maybe even win a prize! Organizer: Emerald Hills Chess is a Dublin nonprofit that teaches 300 K-12 students in the Tri-Valley, and also holds popular (100-150 player) US Chess-rated monthly tournaments.

## Music lessons with Pleasanton's 1776-era fife and drum children's band

- id: 97912bd91fda
- slug: music-lessons-with-pleasanton-s-1776-era-fife-and-drum-children-s-band-97912b
- date: 2027-03-05

- start: 2027-03-05T17:00:00-07:00
- end: 
- all_day: no
- venue: near Mohr Elementary school, call for details
- address: Pleasanton, CA
- city: Pleasanton
- category: music
- tags: 
- price: 
- free: no
- family_friendly: no
- url: https://www.pleasantonweekly.com/calendar/#!/details/music-lessons-with-pleasanton-s-1776-era-fife-and-drum-children-s-band/19689145/2027-03-05T17
- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Do you want your child (8-16) to experience the fun, education and social camaraderie of being in the marching band---but without the enormous time commitment of a school marching band? Look no further! We have been providing music and leadership education to Pleasanton-area kids since our first lesson on March 19th 2010. The Young American Patriots Fife & Drum Corps "YAPs" is a NON-POLITICAL children's parade band dedicated to perpetuating the music, history & heritage of our nation's founding through musical education & traditional value-based teaching. We teach fife (like a flute) and drum in a small group setting.

## Biz Links Networking Group

- id: dbab8676db2e
- slug: biz-links-networking-group-dbab86

- start: 2027-03-09T12:00:00-07:00
- end:

- city: Pleasanton
- category: other
- tags:

- url: https://www.pleasantonweekly.com/calendar/#!/details/biz-links-networking-group/19006731/2027-03-09T12
- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Biz Links is a Chamber networking group focused on building meaningful business relationships, increasing visibility, exchanging referrals, and supporting fellow professionals in the community. Members enjoy valuable networking, engaging conversation, and opportunities to grow both personally and professionally in a welcoming environment. Biz Links meets on the 2nd Tuesday of each month at 12:00 PM at the Pleasanton Chamber of Commerce. Chamber membership is required to join the group, but guests are always welcome to come check us out. Whether you're a longtime member or new to networking, Biz Links is a great place to connect, build relationships, and grow with other local professionals.

Our weekly casual chess nights are aimed at adults (19+) of all levels, including: - passionate online players looking to connect and compete in person - chess parents that want to keep up with their kids - novices that are curious about the game - come between 5:30-6:30 PM to get instruction and tips! ## Music lessons with Pleasanton's 1776-era fife and drum children's band

- category: music
- tags: 
- price: 
- free: no
- family_friendly: no
- url: https://www.pleasantonweekly.com/calendar/#!/details/music-lessons-with-pleasanton-s-1776-era-fife-and-drum-children-s-band/19689145/2027-04-02T17
- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Do you want your child (8-16) to experience the fun, education and social camaraderie of being in the marching band---but without the enormous time commitment of a school marching band? Look no further! We have been providing music and leadership education to Pleasanton-area kids since our first lesson on March 19th 2010. The Young American Patriots Fife & Drum Corps "YAPs" is a NON-POLITICAL children's parade band dedicated to perpetuating the music, history & heritage of our nation's founding through musical education & traditional value-based teaching. We teach fife (like a flute) and drum in a small group setting.

## Biz Links Networking Group

- id: a7d5d9cc7ff4
- slug: biz-links-networking-group-a7d5d9
- date: 2027-04-13
- when: Tuesday, April 13, 2027, 12:00 PM
- start: 2027-04-13T12:00:00-07:00
- end: 
- all_day: no
- venue: Pleasanton Chamber of Commerce
- address: Pleasanton, CA
- city: Pleasanton
- category: other
- tags: 
- price: 
- free: no
- family_friendly: no
- url: https://www.pleasantonweekly.com/calendar/#!/details/biz-links-networking-group/19006731/2027-04-13T12
- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Biz Links is a Chamber networking group focused on building meaningful business relationships, increasing visibility, exchanging referrals, and supporting fellow professionals in the community. Members enjoy valuable networking, engaging conversation, and opportunities to grow both personally and professionally in a welcoming environment. Biz Links meets on the 2nd Tuesday of each month at 12:00 PM at the Pleasanton Chamber of Commerce. Chamber membership is required to join the group, but guests are always welcome to come check us out. Whether you're a longtime member or new to networking, Biz Links is a great place to connect, build relationships, and grow with other local professionals.

## Music lessons with Pleasanton's 1776-era fife and drum children's band

- id: 0953d49bf612
- slug: music-lessons-with-pleasanton-s-1776-era-fife-and-drum-children-s-band-0953d4
- date: 2027-04-30
- when: Friday, April 30, 2027, 5:00 PM
- start: 2027-04-30T17:00:00-07:00
- end: 
- all_day: no
- venue: near Mohr Elementary school, call for details
- address: Pleasanton, CA
- city: Pleasanton
- category: music
- tags: 
- price: 
- free: no
- family_friendly: no
- url: https://www.pleasantonweekly.com/calendar/#!/details/music-lessons-with-pleasanton-s-1776-era-fife-and-drum-children-s-band/19689145/2027-04-30T17
- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Do you want your child (8-16) to experience the fun, education and social camaraderie of being in the marching band---but without the enormous time commitment of a school marching band? Look no further! We have been providing music and leadership education to Pleasanton-area kids since our first lesson on March 19th 2010. The Young American Patriots Fife & Drum Corps "YAPs" is a NON-POLITICAL children's parade band dedicated to perpetuating the music, history & heritage of our nation's founding through musical education & traditional value-based teaching. We teach fife (like a flute) and drum in a small group setting.

## Biz Links Networking Group

- id: 1ebe888c31f7
- slug: biz-links-networking-group-1ebe88
- date: 2027-05-11
- when: Tuesday, May 11, 2027, 12:00 PM
- start: 2027-05-11T12:00:00-07:00
- end: 
- all_day: no
- venue: Pleasanton Chamber of Commerce
- address: Pleasanton, CA
- city: Pleasanton
- category: other
- tags: 
- price: 
- free: no
- family_friendly: no
- url: https://www.pleasantonweekly.com/calendar/#!/details/biz-links-networking-group/19006731/2027-05-11T12
- tickets: 
- source: Pleasanton Weekly

Biz Links is a Chamber networking group focused on building meaningful business relationships, increasing visibility, exchanging referrals, and supporting fellow professionals in the community. Members enjoy valuable networking, engaging conversation, and opportunities to grow both personally and professionally in a welcoming environment. Biz Links meets on the 2nd Tuesday of each month at 12:00 PM at the Pleasanton Chamber of Commerce. Chamber membership is required to join the group, but guests are always welcome to come check us out. Whether you're a longtime member or new to networking, Biz Links is a great place to connect, build relationships, and grow with other local professionals.

Organizer: Emerald Hills Chess is a Dublin nonprofit that teaches 300 K-12 students in the Tri-Valley, and also holds popular (100-150 player) US Chess-rated monthly tournaments.

Biz Links is a Chamber networking group focused on building meaningful business relationships, increasing visibility, exchanging referrals, and supporting fellow professionals in the community. Members enjoy valuable networking, engaging conversation, and opportunities to grow both personally and professionally in a welcoming environment. Biz Links meets on the 2nd Tuesday of each month at 12:00 PM at the Pleasanton Chamber of Commerce. Chamber membership is required to join the group, but guests are always welcome to come check us out. Whether you're a longtime member or new to networking, Biz Links is a great place to connect, build relationships, and grow with other local professionals.
